            className="w-full sm:w-auto h-12 rounded-xl text-base font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 bg-primary/10 border-primary/30 text-primary hover:bg-primary/15"
          >
            <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[2] text-primary" />
            {isEditing ? "معاينة التعديلات" : "معاينة الخطة"}
          </Button>
        </div>

        {hasPreview && preview && (
          <div className="bg-primary/5 border border-primary/15 rounded-2xl p-6 space-y-4">
            <div className="text-center space-y-1">
              <h4 className="font-bold text-foreground">{preview.name}</h4>
              {planningMode === "by_pace" && (
                <p className="text-xs text-primary font-semibold">
                  تاريخ الانتهاء المحسوب: {formatArabicDate(preview.endDate, { includeYear: true })}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 text-center">
              <div><p className="text-2xl font-bold text-primary">{toArabic(preview.totalPages)}</p><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">صفحة إجمالي</p></div>
              <div><p className="text-2xl font-bold text-primary">{toArabic(preview.workDays)}</p><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{labels.workDayLabel}</p></div>
              <div><p className="text-2xl font-bold text-accent">{toArabic(preview.dailyVerses)}</p><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">آية/{labels.workDayLabel}</p></div>
              <div><p className="text-2xl font-bold text-foreground">{toArabic(preview.totalDays)}</p><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">يوم إجمالي</p></div>
              <div><p className="text-2xl font-bold text-muted-foreground">{toArabic(preview.offDayCount)}</p><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">يوم إجازة</p></div>
              <div><p className="text-2xl font-bold text-accent">{toArabic(preview.reviewDays || 0)}</p><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{labels.consolidationDayLabel}</p></div>
            </div>
            <Button onClick={handleSubmit} className="w-full h-12 rounded-xl text-base font-semibold">
              {isEditing ? "حفظ التعديلات" : `بدء ${labels.planTypeName}`}
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


--- FILE: /src/components/quran/Dashboard.jsx ---

// src/components/quran/Dashboard.jsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { Target, Trash2, BookOpen, Layers, Plus, ChevronDown, Pencil, BookmarkCheck, RefreshCw, Check, SlidersHorizontal } from "lucide-react";
import {
  getStats,
  getProgress,
  getTodayData,
  recordDayProgressFlexible,
  skipDayProgress,
  undoDayProgress,
  formatQuranUnits,
  smartQuranDisplay,
  versesToPages,
  toArabicNumbers,
  getPlanLabels,
} from "@/lib/quranPlanEngine";
import ProgressRing from "./ProgressRing";
import StatCard from "./StatCard";
import ScheduleView from "./ScheduleView";
import FlexibleEntryPanel from "./FlexibleEntryPanel";
import QuranDetailsPopover from "./QuranDetailsPopover";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  AlertDialog, AlertDialogAction, AlertDialogCancel, AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription, AlertDialogFooter, AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle, AlertDialogTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import {
  DropdownMenu, DropdownMenuContent, DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu";
import {
  Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipProvider, TooltipTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/tooltip";

function formatArabicUnit(count, singular, dual, plural, accusative) {
  if (count === 1) return singular;
  if (count === 2) return dual;
  const ar = toArabicNumbers(count);
  const mod100 = count % 100;
  if (mod100 >= 3 && mod100 <= 10) return `${ar} ${plural}`;
  return `${ar} ${accusative}`;
}

export default function Dashboard({ plan, allPlans, onPlanUpdate, onDeletePlan, onSwitchPlan, onNewPlan, onEditPlan }) {
  const [recordDayDate, setRecordDayDate] = useState(null);
  const [dialogOpen, setDialogOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isDark, setIsDark] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const checkDark = () => setIsDark(document.documentElement.classList.contains('dark'));
    checkDark();
    const observer = new MutationObserver(checkDark);
    observer.observe(document.documentElement, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] });
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, []);

  const tooltipColors = {
    bg: isDark ? 'hsl(var(--popover))' : '#ffffff',
    text: isDark ? 'hsl(var(--popover-foreground))' : '#1e293b',
    border: isDark ? 'hsl(var(--border))' : '#e2e8f0',
  };

  if (!plan || !plan.schedule || !Array.isArray(plan.schedule)) {
    return (
      <div className="text-center py-12">
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">الخطة غير مكتملة. يرجى إنشاء خطة جديدة.</p>
        <Button type="button" onClick={onNewPlan} className="mt-4">إنشاء خطة جديدة</Button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const planType = plan.planType || "review";
  const labels = getPlanLabels(planType);
  const stats = getStats(plan);
  const progress = getProgress(plan);
  const todayData = getTodayData(plan);
  const alreadyRecorded = todayData && todayData.status !== "pending";
  const dailyPages = plan.dailyPages ?? Math.ceil((plan.totalPages || 0) / Math.max(1, plan.workDays || 1));

  const activeRecordDay = recordDayDate
    ? (plan.schedule.find((d) => d.date === recordDayDate) || null)
    : null;

  const handleRecordDay = (day) => {
    if (!day?.date) return;
    setRecordDayDate(day.date);
    setDialogOpen(true);
  };

  const handleQuickComplete = (day) => {
    if (!day || day.isOff || !(day.targetVerses > 0)) return;
    const refStart = day.targetStartPage || plan.startPage || 1;
    let autoNote = day.note || "";
    if (day.isReviewDay && !autoNote && day.targetStartPage) {
      autoNote = !day.targetEndPage || day.targetStartPage === day.targetEndPage
        ? `${labels.consolidationNoun} صفحة\u00A0${toArabicNumbers(day.targetStartPage)}`
        : `${labels.consolidationNoun} ص\u00A0${toArabicNumbers(day.targetStartPage)} إلى ص\u00A0${toArabicNumbers(day.targetEndPage)}`;
    }

    const updatedPlan = recordDayProgressFlexible(
      plan,
      day.date,
      { juz: 0, hizb: 0, rub: 0, pages: 0, verses: day.targetVerses, customStartPage: refStart },
      autoNote
    );

    const amountLabel = formatQuranUnits(day.targetVerses, refStart);
    onPlanUpdate(updatedPlan, {
      type: "success",
      title: day.isReviewDay ? `تم تسجيل ${labels.consolidationNoun} بالكامل` : "تم إتمام ورد اليوم بنجاح",
      description: `تم تسجيل ${amountLabel}.`,
      action: {
        label: "تراجع",
        onClick: () => {
          const revertedPlan = undoDayProgress(updatedPlan, day.date);
          onPlanUpdate(revertedPlan, {
            type: "info",
            title: "تم التراجع عن التسجيل",
            description: "أُعيد اليوم إلى حالة الانتظار.",
          });
        },
      },
    });
  };

  const handleSaveProgress = (dateStr, calculatedVerses, flexibleInput, note) => {
    const updatedPlan = recordDayProgressFlexible(plan, dateStr, flexibleInput, note);
    onPlanUpdate(updatedPlan);
  };

  const handleSkipProgress = (dateStr) => {
    const updatedPlan = skipDayProgress(plan, dateStr);
    onPlanUpdate(updatedPlan);
  };

  const handleUndoProgress = (dateStr) => {
    const updatedPlan = undoDayProgress(plan, dateStr);
    onPlanUpdate(updatedPlan);
  };

  const completedPages = plan.completedVerses ? versesToPages(plan.completedVerses, plan.startPage) : 0;
  const totalPages = plan.totalPages || 1;
  const canQuickCompleteToday = todayData && !todayData.isOff && !alreadyRecorded && (todayData.targetVerses || 0) > 0;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="flex-1">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-wrap">
            <DropdownMenu dir="rtl">
              <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
                <button type="button" className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-card border border-border/60 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 hover:border-primary/40 transition-colors">
                  {labels.isMemorization ? (
                    <BookmarkCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-primary shrink-0" />
                  ) : (
                    <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4 text-primary shrink-0" />
                  )}
                  <span className="font-bold text-foreground text-sm max-w-[180px] sm:max-w-[240px] truncate pe-1 leading-normal">{plan.name || "بدون اسم"}</span>
                  <span className="text-[11px] bg-primary/15 text-primary border border-primary/25 font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full leading-normal">
                    {labels.planTypeName}
                  </span>
                  <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground shrink-0" />
                </button>
              </DropdownMenuTrigger>
              <DropdownMenuContent align="start" className="w-80 p-1.5 rounded-2xl">
                {allPlans.map((p) => {
                  const pType = p.planType || "review";
                  const pLabels = getPlanLabels(pType);
                  const isActive = p.id === plan.id;
                  const isSameTheme = pType === planType;

                  return (
                    <DropdownMenuItem
                      key={p.id}
                      onClick={() => !isActive && onSwitchPlan(p.id)}
                      className={`group flex items-center justify-between gap-2 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
                        isActive
                          ? "bg-primary/10 focus:bg-primary/20 focus:text-foreground"
                          : "focus:bg-primary/15 focus:text-foreground"
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span className="truncate flex-1 text-right font-medium text-foreground pe-1 leading-normal">
                        {p.name}
                      </span>

                      <span
                        className={`text-[10px] font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border shrink-0 leading-normal transition-colors ${
                          isSameTheme
                            ? "bg-primary/15 text-primary border-primary/25"
                            : "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground border-border"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {pLabels.planTypeName}
                      </span>

                      {isActive && (
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold bg-primary text-primary-foreground px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full shrink-0 leading-normal shadow-xs">
                          نشط
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </DropdownMenuItem>
                  );
                })}
                <div className="border-t border-border/40 mt-1.5 pt-1.5">
                  <DropdownMenuItem
                    onClick={onNewPlan}
                    className="rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-primary font-semibold cursor-pointer focus:bg-primary/15 focus:text-primary"
                  >
                    <Plus className="w-4 h-4 me-2 shrink-0" /> خطة جديدة
                  </DropdownMenuItem>
                </div>
              </DropdownMenuContent>
            </DropdownMenu>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <TooltipProvider>
                <Tooltip>
                  <TooltipTrigger asChild>
                    <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => onEditPlan(plan)} className="h-10 w-10 p-0 border-primary/25 hover:bg-primary/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                      <Pencil className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                    </Button>
                  </TooltipTrigger>
                  <TooltipContent side="bottom" className="text-xs border" style={{ backgroundColor: tooltipColors.bg, color: tooltipColors.text, borderColor: tooltipColors.border, borderRadius: '6px', padding: '4px 10px', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                    <p>تعديل الخطة</p>
                  </TooltipContent>
                </Tooltip>
              </TooltipProvider>

              <AlertDialog>
                <TooltipProvider>
                  <Tooltip>
                    <TooltipTrigger asChild>
                      <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
                        <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="sm" className="h-10 w-10 p-0 text-destructive border-destructive/25 hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive flex items-center justify-center">
                          <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                        </Button>
                      </AlertDialogTrigger>
                    </TooltipTrigger>
                    <TooltipContent side="bottom" className="text-xs border" style={{ backgroundColor: tooltipColors.bg, color: tooltipColors.text, borderColor: tooltipColors.border, borderRadius: '6px', padding: '4px 10px', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                      <p>حذف الخطة</p>
                    </TooltipContent>
                  </Tooltip>
                </TooltipProvider>
                <AlertDialogContent dir="rtl">
                  <AlertDialogHeader>
                    <AlertDialogTitle>حذف "{plan.name}"</AlertDialogTitle>
                    <AlertDialogDescription>هل أنت متأكد من حذف هذه الخطة؟ سيتم فقدان جميع بيانات التقدم.</AlertDialogDescription>
                  </AlertDialogHeader>
                  <AlertDialogFooter className="flex gap-2 sm:gap-2">
                    <AlertDialogCancel>إلغاء</AlertDialogCancel>
                    <AlertDialogAction onClick={() => onDeletePlan(plan.id)} className="bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground hover:bg-destructive/90">
                      حذف
                    </AlertDialogAction>
                  </AlertDialogFooter>
                </AlertDialogContent>
              </AlertDialog>
            </div>
          </div>

          <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-2 text-sm flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              {smartQuranDisplay(plan.totalVerses || 0, plan.startPage || 1)}
              <QuranDetailsPopover verses={plan.totalVerses || 0} startPage={plan.startPage || 1} />
            </span>
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 bg-card border border-border/60 rounded-2xl px-5 py-3">
          <div className="text-center">
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">مُنجَز</p>
            <p className="text-lg font-bold text-primary">{toArabicNumbers(completedPages)}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="w-px h-8 bg-border/60" />
          <div className="text-center">
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">متبقي</p>
            <p className="text-lg font-bold text-accent">{toArabicNumbers(Math.max(0, totalPages - completedPages))}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="w-px h-8 bg-border/60" />
          <div className="text-center">
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">يوميًا</p>
            <p className="text-lg font-bold text-primary">{toArabicNumbers(dailyPages)}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        <div className="bg-card rounded-2xl border border-border/60 p-5 sm:p-6 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          <ProgressRing progress={progress || 0} />
          <div className="mt-4 text-center space-y-1">
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              <span className="font-bold text-foreground">{toArabicNumbers(completedPages)}</span> من {toArabicNumbers(totalPages)} صفحة
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              متبقي: <span className="font-semibold text-accent">{toArabicNumbers(Math.max(0, totalPages - completedPages))} صفحة</span>
            </p>
            {(plan.completedVerses || 0) > 0 && (
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{formatQuranUnits(plan.completedVerses, plan.startPage || 1)}</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {todayData && (
          // ✅ ضبط الحشوة الداخلية على الهاتف (p-4 sm:p-6) مع min-w-0 لمنع تجاوز الحواف
          <div className={`rounded-2xl border p-4 sm:p-6 min-w-0 ${
            todayData.isOff
              ? 'bg-secondary/50 border-border/40'
              : 'bg-primary/5 border-primary/20'
          }`}>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-3">
              <h3 className="font-bold text-foreground flex items-center gap-2">
                {todayData.isReviewDay ? (
                  <RefreshCw className="w-5 h-5 text-primary shrink-0" />
                ) : (
                  <Target className="w-5 h-5 text-primary shrink-0" />
                )}
                <span>مهمة اليوم</span>
              </h3>
              {todayData.isReviewDay && (
                <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-accent/15 text-accent border border-accent/25">
                  {labels.consolidationNoun}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            {todayData.isOff ? (
              <div className="text-center py-4 space-y-3">
                <p className="text-lg font-semibold text-muted-foreground">يوم إجازة</p>
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">استمتع بيومك!</p>
                <Button type="button" onClick={() => handleRecordDay(todayData)} variant="outline" className="rounded-xl" size="sm">
                  تسجيل إنجاز استثنائي
                </Button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <>
                <div className="text-center py-2 space-y-2">
                  <p className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-primary leading-snug">
                    {todayData.targetVerses > 0
                      ? formatQuranUnits(todayData.targetVerses, todayData.targetStartPage || plan.startPage || 1)
                      : labels.consolidationNoun}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                    {todayData.isReviewDay ? `مقترح ${labels.consolidationNoun} (الدورة السابقة)` : `ورد ${labels.workNoun} المطلوب`}
                  </p>
                  {todayData.targetStartPage && (
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground bg-muted/50 rounded-lg py-1.5 px-3 inline-block">
                      {!todayData.targetEndPage || todayData.targetStartPage === todayData.targetEndPage
                        ? `صفحة\u00A0${toArabicNumbers(todayData.targetStartPage)}`
                        : `من ص\u00A0${toArabicNumbers(todayData.targetStartPage)} إلى ص\u00A0${toArabicNumbers(todayData.targetEndPage)}`}
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* ✅ تخطيط مرن متماثل الهوامش على الهاتف مع التفاف آمن عند تكبير الخط */}
                {canQuickCompleteToday ? (
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mt-4 w-full">
                    <Button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleQuickComplete(todayData)}
                      className="flex-[3_1_150px] min-w-0 rounded-xl h-10 sm:h-11 px-3 sm:px-4 text-sm font-semibold gap-1.5"
                    >
                      <Check className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate">
                        {todayData.isReviewDay
                          ? (labels.isMemorization ? "أتممت التثبيت" : "أتممت الربط")
                          : "أتممت الورد"}
                      </span>
                    </Button>
                    <Button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleRecordDay(todayData)}
                      variant="outline"
                      className="flex-[2_1_95px] sm:flex-none min-w-0 rounded-xl h-10 sm:h-11 px-3 sm:px-4 text-sm font-semibold border-primary/30 text-primary hover:bg-primary/10 hover:text-primary gap-1.5"
                    >
                      <SlidersHorizontal className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                      <span>تخصيص</span>
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <Button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleRecordDay(todayData)}
                    className="w-full mt-4 rounded-xl h-10 sm:h-11 text-sm font-semibold"
                    variant={alreadyRecorded ? "outline" : "default"}
                  >
                    {todayData.status === "skipped"
                      ? "تم التخطي · تعديل"
                      : alreadyRecorded
                      ? `تعديل (${formatQuranUnits(todayData.completedVerses || 0, todayData.targetStartPage || plan.startPage || 1)})`
                      : todayData.isReviewDay
                      ? `تسجيل ${labels.consolidationNoun}`
                      : "تسجيل الإنجاز"}
                  </Button>
                )}
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="space-y-3 flex flex-col justify-center">
          <StatCard
            icon={labels.isMemorization ? BookmarkCheck : BookOpen}
            label="المقدار اليومي الحالي"
            value={formatArabicUnit(dailyPages, "صفحة واحدة", "صفحتان", "صفحات", "صفحة")}
            subValue={`يتحدث تلقائياً بناءً على المتبقي من ${labels.workNoun}`}
          />
          <StatCard
            icon={Layers}
            label="الأيام المتبقية"
            value={formatArabicUnit(stats?.pendingDays || 0, "يوم واحد", "يومان", "أيام", "يوماً")}
            subValue={`${toArabicNumbers(stats?.completedDays || 0)} منجز · ${toArabicNumbers(stats?.partialDays || 0)} جزئي · ${toArabicNumbers(stats?.skippedDays || 0)} متخطى`}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <ScheduleView
        schedule={plan.schedule || []}
        onRecordDay={handleRecordDay}
        onQuickComplete={handleQuickComplete}
        planType={planType}
      />
      <FlexibleEntryPanel
        open={dialogOpen}
        onOpenChange={(nextOpen) => {
          setDialogOpen(nextOpen);
          if (!nextOpen) setRecordDayDate(null);
        }}
        day={activeRecordDay}
        planType={planType}
        onSave={handleSaveProgress}
        onSkip={handleSkipProgress}
        onUndo={handleUndoProgress}
        alreadyRecorded={activeRecordDay && activeRecordDay.status !== "pending"}
      />
    </div>
  );
}


--- FILE: /src/components/quran/DayCard.jsx ---

// src/components/quran/DayCard.jsx
import React from "react";
import { Check, X, Clock, Coffee, RefreshCw, ArrowLeft, SlidersHorizontal } from "lucide-react";
import { formatQuranUnits, toArabicNumbers, formatArabicDate, getPlanLabels } from "@/lib/quranPlanEngine";
import {
  Tooltip,
  TooltipContent,
  TooltipProvider,
  TooltipTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/tooltip";

const statusConfig = {
  completed: { icon: Check, bg: "bg-primary/10", text: "text-primary", border: "border-primary/20", label: "مُنجَز" },
  partial: { icon: Clock, bg: "bg-accent/10", text: "text-accent", border: "border-accent/20", label: "جزئي" },
  skipped: { icon: X, bg: "bg-destructive/10", text: "text-destructive", border: "border-destructive/20", label: "مُتخطّى" },
  pending: { icon: Clock, bg: "bg-muted/50", text: "text-muted-foreground", border: "border-border", label: "قادم" },
};

function PageRangeDisplay({ startPage, endPage }) {
  if (!startPage) return null;
  if (!endPage || startPage === endPage) {
    return (
      <span className="inline-flex items-center text-xs text-muted-foreground font-medium bg-muted/50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md">
        صفحة{"\u00A0"}{toArabicNumbers(startPage)}
      </span>
    );
  }
  return (
    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground font-medium bg-muted/50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md">
      <span>ص{"\u00A0"}{toArabicNumbers(startPage)}</span>
      <ArrowLeft className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" />
      <span>ص{"\u00A0"}{toArabicNumbers(endPage)}</span>
    </span>
  );
}

export default function DayCard({ day, isToday, onRecord, onQuickComplete, planType = "review" }) {
  if (!day) return null;

  const labels = getPlanLabels(planType);
  const consolidationProposalLabel = labels.isMemorization ? "مقترح التثبيت" : "مقترح الربط";
  const canQuickComplete = isToday && day.status === "pending" && (day.targetVerses || 0) > 0 && !!onQuickComplete;

  // 1. يوم الإجازة الأسبوعية (راحة تامة)
  if (day.isOff) {
    if (isToday) {
      return (
        <div className="col-span-full bg-secondary/45 rounded-2xl border border-border/50 p-4 sm:p-5 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-muted flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
              <Coffee className="w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />
            </div>
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                <span className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground">{day.dayName}</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">· {formatArabicDate(day.date)}</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground bg-muted px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-medium">
                  إجازة اليوم
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">يوم راحة تامة — استمتع بيومك!</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          {onRecord && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onRecord(day)}
              className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-background/70 py-2 px-3.5 text-xs font-semibold text-foreground transition-colors hover:border-primary/40 hover:text-primary shrink-0"
            >
              تسجيل إنجاز استثنائي
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      );
    }

    return (
      <div className="h-full bg-secondary/35 rounded-2xl border border-border/40 p-4 flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 opacity-85">
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
          <TooltipProvider>
            <Tooltip>
              <TooltipTrigger asChild>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 cursor-default min-w-0">
                  <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-muted/80 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                    <Coffee className="w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-muted-foreground leading-tight">{day.dayName}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground/80 mt-0.5">{formatArabicDate(day.date)}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </TooltipTrigger>
              <TooltipContent side="top" className="text-xs">
                يوم راحة تامة
              </TooltipContent>
            </Tooltip>
          </TooltipProvider>
          <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground bg-muted px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full shrink-0">
            إجازة
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="pt-2 border-t border-border/30">
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">يوم راحة تامة</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // 2. يوم التثبيت / الربط والتجميع
  if (day.isReviewDay) {
    const config = statusConfig[day.status] || statusConfig.pending;
    const recordedAmount = day.consolidationVerses || day.completedVerses || 0;

    if (isToday) {
      return (
        <div className="col-span-full rounded-2xl border p-4 sm:p-5 bg-primary/5 border-primary/30 shadow-sm shadow-primary/5 ring-1 ring-primary/10 transition-all duration-300 min-w-0">
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4">
            <div className="space-y-2.5 min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between sm:justify-start gap-2.5 flex-wrap">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                  <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-primary text-primary-foreground flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                    <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground">{day.dayName}</span>
                    <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground ms-2">{formatArabicDate(day.date)}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <span className="rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold bg-primary/15 text-primary border border-primary/25">
                    اليوم
                  </span>
                  <span className="rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold bg-accent/15 text-accent border border-accent/25">
                    {labels.consolidationNoun}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {day.targetVerses > 0 ? (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{consolidationProposalLabel}:</span>
                  <span className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-foreground">
                    {formatQuranUnits(day.targetVerses, day.targetStartPage || 1)}
                  </span>
                  <PageRangeDisplay startPage={day.targetStartPage} endPage={day.targetEndPage} />
                </div>
              ) : (
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">اختر ما تريد تثبيته من المصحف</p>
              )}

              {day.note && (
                <div className="text-xs text-primary font-medium bg-primary/10 rounded-lg py-1 px-2.5 inline-block max-w-full truncate leading-normal">
                  {day.note}
                </div>
              )}

              {day.status !== "pending" && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs pt-1">
                  <span className="text-muted-foreground">الحالة:</span>
                  <span className={`font-bold ${config.text}`}>
                    {day.status === "skipped"
                      ? "مُتخطّى"
                      : recordedAmount > 0
                      ? formatQuranUnits(recordedAmount, day.targetStartPage || 1)
                      : config.label}
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* ✅ تخطيط مرن للأزرار يمنع تجاوز الحواف على شاشات الهواتف الضيقة */}
            {onRecord && (
              <div className="w-full sm:w-auto sm:min-w-[235px] shrink-0">
                {canQuickComplete ? (
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onQuickComplete(day)}
                      className="flex-[3_1_150px] min-w-0 rounded-xl bg-primary h-10 px-3 sm:px-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-foreground shadow-sm transition-all duration-200 hover:bg-primary/90 active:scale-[0.98] flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                    >
                      <Check className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate">{labels.isMemorization ? "أتممت التثبيت" : "أتممت الربط"}</span>
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onRecord(day)}
                      className="flex-[2_1_95px] sm:flex-none min-w-0 rounded-xl border border-primary/30 bg-background/70 h-10 px-3 text-sm font-semibold text-primary transition-colors hover:bg-primary/10 active:scale-[0.98] flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                    >
                      <SlidersHorizontal className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                      <span>تخصيص</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onRecord(day)}
                    className="w-full rounded-xl bg-primary h-10 px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-foreground shadow-sm transition-all duration-200 hover:bg-primary/90 active:scale-[0.98]"
                  >
                    {day.status !== "pending" ? `تعديل ${labels.consolidationNoun}` : `تسجيل ${labels.consolidationNoun}`}
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return (
      <div
        className={`h-full rounded-2xl border p-4 flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 transition-all duration-300 ${
          day.status === "completed"
            ? "bg-primary/10 border-primary/25"
            : `${config.bg} ${config.border}`
        }`}
      >
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
            <div className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${
              day.status === "completed" ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground" : "bg-primary/15 text-primary"
            }`}>
              <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </div>
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-foreground leading-tight">{day.dayName}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">{formatArabicDate(day.date)}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <span className="rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold bg-accent/15 text-accent border border-accent/25 shrink-0">
            {labels.consolidationNoun}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-2 border-t border-border/35">
          {day.targetVerses > 0 ? (
            <>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{consolidationProposalLabel}</p>
              <p className="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-foreground leading-snug">
                {formatQuranUnits(day.targetVerses, day.targetStartPage || 1)}
              </p>
              <div className="pt-0.5">
                <PageRangeDisplay startPage={day.targetStartPage} endPage={day.targetEndPage} />
              </div>
            </>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">اختر ما تريد تثبيته من المصحف</p>
          )}

          {day.note && (
            <div className="text-[11px] text-primary font-medium mt-1 bg-primary/10 rounded-lg py-1 px-2 truncate leading-normal">
              {day.note}
            </div>
          )}

          {day.status !== "pending" && (
            <div className="pt-2 mt-1 border-t border-border/40 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
              <span className="text-muted-foreground">الحالة:</span>
              <span className={`font-bold ${config.text}`}>
                {day.status === "skipped"
                  ? "مُتخطّى"
                  : recordedAmount > 0
                  ? formatQuranUnits(recordedAmount, day.targetStartPage || 1)
                  : config.label}
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // 3. يوم الحفظ / المراجعة الأساسي
  const config = statusConfig[day.status] || statusConfig.pending;
  const StatusIcon = config.icon;
  const isTodayPending = isToday && day.status === "pending";

  if (isToday) {
    return (
      <div className="col-span-full rounded-2xl border p-4 sm:p-5 bg-primary/5 border-primary/30 shadow-sm shadow-primary/5 ring-1 ring-primary/10 transition-all duration-300 min-w-0">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="space-y-2.5 min-w-0 flex-1">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between sm:justify-start gap-2.5 flex-wrap">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-primary text-primary-foreground flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                  <StatusIcon className="w-4 h-4" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground">{day.dayName}</span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground ms-2">{formatArabicDate(day.date)}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              {isTodayPending ? (
                <span className="rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold bg-primary/15 text-primary border border-primary/25">
                  ورد اليوم
                </span>
              ) : (
                <span className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${config.bg} ${config.text}`}>
                  {config.label}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <span className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-foreground">
                {formatQuranUnits(day.targetVerses, day.targetStartPage || 1)}
              </span>
              <PageRangeDisplay startPage={day.targetStartPage} endPage={day.targetEndPage} />
              {day.targetQuarterName && (
                <span className="text-[11px] text-primary font-semibold bg-primary/10 rounded-md py-0.5 px-2 leading-normal">
                  {day.targetQuarterName}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>

            {day.status !== "pending" && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs pt-0.5">
                <span className="text-muted-foreground">المُنجَز:</span>
                <span className={`font-bold ${day.completedVerses >= day.targetVerses ? "text-primary" : "text-accent"}`}>
                  {day.status === "skipped"
                    ? "مُتخطّى"
                    : formatQuranUnits(day.completedVerses, day.targetStartPage || 1)}
                </span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {onRecord && (
            <div className="w-full sm:w-auto sm:min-w-[235px] shrink-0">
              {canQuickComplete ? (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onQuickComplete(day)}
                    className="flex-[3_1_150px] min-w-0 rounded-xl bg-primary h-10 px-3 sm:px-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-foreground shadow-sm transition-all duration-200 hover:bg-primary/90 active:scale-[0.98] flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                  >
                    <Check className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                    <span className="truncate">أتممت الورد</span>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onRecord(day)}
                    className="flex-[2_1_95px] sm:flex-none min-w-0 rounded-xl border border-primary/30 bg-background/70 h-10 px-3 text-sm font-semibold text-primary transition-colors hover:bg-primary/10 active:scale-[0.98] flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                  >
                    <SlidersHorizontal className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                    <span>تخصيص</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onRecord(day)}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl bg-primary h-10 px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-foreground shadow-sm transition-all duration-200 hover:bg-primary/90 active:scale-[0.98]"
                >
                  {day.status !== "pending" ? "تعديل الإنجاز" : "تسجيل الإنجاز"}
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div
      className={`h-full rounded-2xl border p-4 flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 transition-all duration-300 ${config.bg} ${config.border}`}
    >
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
          <div className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${config.bg}`}>
            <StatusIcon className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${config.text}`} />
          </div>
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-foreground leading-tight">{day.dayName}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">{formatArabicDate(day.date)}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <span className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold shrink-0 ${config.bg} ${config.text}`}>
          {config.label}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-2 border-t border-border/35">
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">الورد</p>
        <p className="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-foreground leading-snug">
          {formatQuranUnits(day.targetVerses, day.targetStartPage || 1)}
        </p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 pt-0.5">
          <PageRangeDisplay startPage={day.targetStartPage} endPage={day.targetEndPage} />
          {day.targetQuarterName && (
            <span className="text-[11px] text-primary font-semibold bg-primary/10 rounded-md py-0.5 px-2 leading-normal">
              {day.targetQuarterName}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        {day.status !== "pending" && (
          <div className="pt-2 mt-1 border-t border-border/40 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
            <span className="text-muted-foreground">المُنجَز:</span>
            <span className={`font-bold ${day.completedVerses >= day.targetVerses ? "text-primary" : "text-accent"}`}>
              {day.status === "skipped"
                ? "مُتخطّى"
                : formatQuranUnits(day.completedVerses, day.targetStartPage || 1)}
            </span>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


--- FILE: /src/components/quran/FlexibleEntryPanel.jsx ---

// src/components/quran/FlexibleEntryPanel.jsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogFooter } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Check, BookOpen, RotateCcw, ChevronUp, ChevronDown, LayoutGrid, Target, SkipForward, RefreshCw, Coffee, ArrowLeft } from "lucide-react";
import {
  formatQuranUnits,
  flexibleInputToVerses,
  versesToPagesCount,
  getVersesBetween,
  toArabicNumbers,
  formatArabicDate,
  getPlanLabels,
} from "@/lib/quranPlanEngine";
import QuranInteractivePicker from "@/components/QuranInteractivePicker";

const normalizeDigits = (val) => {
  const map = {
    "٠": "0", "١": "1", "٢": "2", "٣": "3", "٤": "4",
    "٥": "5", "٦": "6", "٧": "7", "٨": "8", "٩": "9",
    "۰": "0", "۱": "1", "۲": "2", "۳": "3", "۴": "4",
    "۵": "5", "۶": "6", "۷": "7", "۸": "8", "۹": "9",
  };
  return String(val).replace(/[٠-٩۰-۹]/g, (d) => map[d] || d);
};

export default function FlexibleEntryPanel({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  day,
  planType = "review",
  onSave,
  onSkip,
  onUndo,
  alreadyRecorded,
}) {
  const [input, setInput] = useState({ juz: 0, hizb: 0, rub: 0, pages: 0, verses: 0, customStartPage: null });
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
  const [useVisualMode, setUseVisualMode] = useState(false);
  const [guidedFilled, setGuidedFilled] = useState(false);
  const [guidedFillCount, setGuidedFillCount] = useState(0);
  const [guidedClearCount, setGuidedClearCount] = useState(0);

  const labels = getPlanLabels(planType);
  const consolidationProposalLabel = labels.isMemorization ? "مقترح التثبيت" : "مقترح الربط";

  useEffect(() => {
    if (day) {
      setNote(day.note || "");
      const existingVerses = day.isReviewDay
        ? (day.consolidationVerses || day.completedVerses || 0)
        : (day.completedVerses || 0);

      if (existingVerses > 0) {
        setInput({
          juz: 0,
          hizb: 0,
          rub: 0,
          pages: 0,
          verses: existingVerses,
          customStartPage: day.targetStartPage || 1,
        });
      } else {
        setInput({ juz: 0, hizb: 0, rub: 0, pages: 0, verses: 0, customStartPage: null });
      }
    }
    setGuidedFilled(false);
    setGuidedFillCount(0);
    setGuidedClearCount(0);
  }, [day, open]);

  const startPage = input.customStartPage || day?.targetStartPage || 1;
  const calculatedVerses = flexibleInputToVerses(input, startPage);
  const targetVerses = day?.targetVerses || 0;
  const targetPages = day?.targetStartPage && day?.targetEndPage
    ? (day.targetEndPage - day.targetStartPage + 1)
    : versesToPagesCount(targetVerses, day?.targetStartPage || 1);

  const diff = day ? (calculatedVerses - targetVerses) : 0;

  // ✅ تبديل التعبئة التلقائية: يفكك الصفحات الكاملة بدقة أو الآيات الدقيقة، ويزيل التحديد عند الضغط مرة أخرى
  const toggleGuidedFill = () => {
    if (!day || (day.targetVerses <= 0 && (!day.targetStartPage || !day.targetEndPage))) return;

    if (guidedFilled) {
      setInput({ juz: 0, hizb: 0, rub: 0, pages: 0, verses: 0, customStartPage: null });
      setNote(day.note || "");
      setGuidedFilled(false);
      setGuidedClearCount((c) => c + 1);
      return;
    }

    const refStart = day.targetStartPage || 1;
    const hasPageRange = !!(day.targetStartPage && day.targetEndPage);
    const exactRangeVerses = hasPageRange ? getVersesBetween(day.targetStartPage, day.targetEndPage) : 0;

    if (hasPageRange && (day.targetVerses === 0 || day.targetVerses === exactRangeVerses)) {
      const totalPagesCount = Math.max(1, day.targetEndPage - day.targetStartPage + 1);
      const juzCount = Math.floor(totalPagesCount / 20);
      const remPages = totalPagesCount % 20;
      const hizbCount = Math.floor(remPages / 10);
      const directPages = remPages % 10;

      setInput({
        juz: juzCount,
        hizb: hizbCount,
        rub: 0,
        pages: directPages,
        verses: 0,
        customStartPage: refStart,
      });
    } else {
      setInput({
        juz: 0,
        hizb: 0,
        rub: 0,
        pages: 0,
        verses: day.targetVerses,
        customStartPage: refStart,
      });
    }

    if (day.isReviewDay && day.targetStartPage) {
      const rangeText = !day.targetEndPage || day.targetStartPage === day.targetEndPage
        ? `صفحة\u00A0${toArabicNumbers(day.targetStartPage)}`
        : `ص\u00A0${toArabicNumbers(day.targetStartPage)} إلى ص\u00A0${toArabicNumbers(day.targetEndPage)}`;
      setNote(`${labels.consolidationNoun} ${rangeText}`);
    }

    setGuidedFilled(true);
    setGuidedFillCount((c) => c + 1);
  };

  const isLess = diff < 0 && calculatedVerses > 0;
  const isMore = diff > 0;

  const getDiffDisplay = () => {
    const absDiff = Math.max(0, Math.abs(diff));
    if (absDiff < 15) return `${toArabicNumbers(absDiff)} آية`;
    const totalInputPages = (input.juz || 0) * 20 + (input.hizb || 0) * 10 + (input.rub || 0) * 2.5 + (input.pages || 0);
    const pageDiffAbs = Math.abs(totalInputPages - targetPages);
    if (input.verses > 0) {
      if (isLess) return `${toArabicNumbers(Math.ceil(absDiff / 15))} صفحة تقريباً (أو ${toArabicNumbers(absDiff)} آية)`;
      else if (isMore) return `${toArabicNumbers(Math.floor(pageDiffAbs))} صفحة و ${toArabicNumbers(input.verses)} آية`;
    }
    return `${toArabicNumbers(Math.max(1, Math.round(absDiff / 15)))} صفحة`;
  };

  const resetInput = () => {
    setInput({ juz: 0, hizb: 0, rub: 0, pages: 0, verses: 0, customStartPage: null });
    setNote("");
    setGuidedFilled(false);
    setGuidedFillCount(0);
    setGuidedClearCount(0);
  };

  const handleSave = () => {
    if (calculatedVerses === 0) return;
    onSave(day.date, calculatedVerses, input, note.trim());
    onOpenChange(false);
    resetInput();
  };

  const handleSkip = () => {
    if (onSkip) {
      onSkip(day.date);
    }
    onOpenChange(false);
    resetInput();
  };

  const handleUndo = () => {
    onUndo(day.date);
    onOpenChange(false);
    resetInput();
  };

  const adjustValue = (key, amount) => {
    setGuidedFilled(false);
    setInput(prev => ({ ...prev, [key]: Math.max(0, (prev[key] || 0) + amount) }));
  };

  if (!day) return null;

  const canQuickFill = !day.isOff && (day.targetVerses > 0 || (day.targetStartPage && day.targetEndPage));

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent
        className="w-[calc(100vw-1.5rem)] max-w-md max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden p-4 sm:p-6 rounded-3xl"
        dir="rtl"
        onOpenAutoFocus={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
      >
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle className="text-center font-heading text-xl text-foreground">
            {day.isReviewDay
              ? (alreadyRecorded ? `تعديل ${labels.consolidationNoun}` : `تسجيل ${labels.consolidationNoun}`)
              : (alreadyRecorded ? "تعديل إنجاز اليوم" : "تسجيل إنجاز اليوم")}
          </DialogTitle>
        </DialogHeader>

        <div className="py-2 sm:py-3 space-y-4 sm:space-y-5 min-w-0 w-full">
          <div className="text-center bg-muted/50 rounded-xl p-3.5 sm:p-4">
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              {day.dayName} - {formatArabicDate(day.date)}
            </p>
            {day.isOff ? (
              <div className="mt-1.5 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                <span className="w-7 h-7 rounded-xl bg-muted flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                  <Coffee className="w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />
                </span>
                <span className="text-lg font-bold text-foreground">يوم إجازة</span>
              </div>
            ) : day.isReviewDay ? (
              <div className="mt-1 space-y-1">
                <p className="text-base font-bold text-foreground flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
                  <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4 text-primary" />
                  <span>{consolidationProposalLabel} (الدورة السابقة):</span>
                </p>
                {targetVerses > 0 ? (
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
                    <span className="text-lg font-bold text-primary">
                      {formatQuranUnits(targetVerses, day.targetStartPage || 1)}
                    </span>
                    {day.targetStartPage && (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">
                        {!day.targetEndPage || day.targetStartPage === day.targetEndPage ? (
                          <span>(صفحة{"\u00A0"}{toArabicNumbers(day.targetStartPage)})</span>
                        ) : (
                          <>
                            <span>(ص{"\u00A0"}{toArabicNumbers(day.targetStartPage)}</span>
                            <ArrowLeft className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" />
                            <span>ص{"\u00A0"}{toArabicNumbers(day.targetEndPage)})</span>
                          </>
                        )}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">اختر أي مقدار تريد تثبيته</p>
                )}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-lg font-bold text-foreground mt-1">
                الورد: <span className="text-primary">{formatQuranUnits(targetVerses, day.targetStartPage || 1)}</span>
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0 w-full">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-3 min-w-0">
              <Label className="text-sm font-semibold text-foreground leading-normal">
                {day.isReviewDay ? `ماذا ثبّتَّ اليوم؟` : `ماذا أنجزت اليوم؟`}
              </Label>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                {canQuickFill && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={toggleGuidedFill}
                    className={`text-xs font-semibold px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap border ${
                      guidedFilled
                        ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground border-primary hover:bg-primary/90"
                        : "bg-primary/10 text-primary border-primary/25 hover:bg-primary/20"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {/* ✅ تصحيح لغوي: "تمت التعبئة" */}
                    {guidedFilled ? (
                      <><Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" /> تمت التعبئة</>
                    ) : (
                      <><Target className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" /> {day.isReviewDay ? "تعبئة المقترح" : "تعبئة الورد"}</>
                    )}
                  </button>
                )}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setUseVisualMode(!useVisualMode)}
                  className="text-xs bg-primary/10 text-primary font-semibold px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1 hover:bg-primary/20 transition-colors shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap"
                >
                  {useVisualMode ? <><Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0"/> إدخال رقمي</> : <><LayoutGrid className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0"/> واجهة المصحف</>}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className={useVisualMode ? "min-w-0 w-full" : "hidden"}>
              <QuranInteractivePicker
                targetStartPage={day?.targetStartPage || 1}
                targetEndPage={day?.targetEndPage || 604}
                planStartPage={1}
                planEndPage={604}
                clearTrigger={guidedClearCount}
                presetRange={
                  guidedFillCount > 0 && day?.targetStartPage && day?.targetEndPage
                    ? {
                        startPage: day.targetStartPage,
                        endPage: day.targetEndPage,
                        triggerKey: guidedFillCount,
                      }
                    : null
                }
                onSelectionChange={(data) => {
                  setGuidedFilled(false);
                  if (data.versesCount > 0) {
                    setInput({
                      juz: 0,
                      hizb: 0,
                      rub: 0,
                      pages: 0,
                      verses: data.versesCount,
                      customStartPage: data.startPage,
                    });
                    if (day.isReviewDay && data.displayLabel) {
                      setNote(data.displayLabel);
                    }
                  } else {
                    setInput({ juz: 0, hizb: 0, rub: 0, pages: 0, verses: 0, customStartPage: null });
                  }
                }}
              />
            </div>

            <div className={useVisualMode ? "hidden" : "min-w-0 w-full"}>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-5 gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 mb-3">
                {[{ key: "juz", label: "أجزاء" }, { key: "hizb", label: "أحزاب" }, { key: "rub", label: "أرباع" }, { key: "pages", label: "صفحات" }, { key: "verses", label: "آيات" }].map((unit) => (
                  <div key={unit.key} className="text-center space-y-1 min-w-0">
                    <Label className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground block">{unit.label}</Label>
                    <div className="relative flex flex-col items-center bg-background border border-border/60 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden focus-within:border-primary/50 transition-colors">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => adjustValue(unit.key, 1)}
                        className="w-full py-1.5 flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground hover:text-primary active:scale-95 transition-colors"
                      >
                        <ChevronUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      </button>
                      <Input
                        type="text"
                        inputMode="numeric"
                        value={toArabicNumbers(input[unit.key] || 0)}
                        onChange={(e) => {
                          setGuidedFilled(false);
                          const normalized = normalizeDigits(e.target.value).replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
                          const val = parseInt(normalized, 10) || 0;
                          setInput(prev => ({ ...prev, [unit.key]: val }));
                        }}
                        className="h-7 w-full border-0 shadow-none bg-transparent p-0 text-center text-sm font-bold text-foreground focus-visible:ring-0"
                        dir="rtl"
                      />
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => adjustValue(unit.key, -1)}
                        className="w-full py-1.5 flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground hover:text-primary active:scale-95 transition-colors"
                      >
                        <ChevronDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {day.isReviewDay && (
              <div className="mt-3">
                <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1.5 block">
                  ملاحظة اختيارية (ما الذي ثبّتَّه؟)
                </Label>
                <Input
                  type="text"
                  value={note}
                  onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="مثال: تثبيت سورة الكهف أو أول ربعين من البقرة..."
                  className="h-10 rounded-xl border-input text-right text-sm text-foreground"
                  dir="rtl"
                />
              </div>
            )}

            {/* ✅ تصغير حجم خط الإجمالي المحسوب ليتناسق مع بقية عناصر النافذة */}
            <div className={`bg-card border rounded-xl p-3.5 text-center mt-3 transition-colors ${
              guidedFilled ? "border-primary/40 bg-primary/5" : "border-border/60"
            }`}>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">الإجمالي المحسوب</p>
              {calculatedVerses > 0 ? (
                <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-primary mt-1 leading-snug">
                  {formatQuranUnits(calculatedVerses, startPage)}
                </p>
              ) : (
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground mt-1.5">
                  لم يُحدد مقدار بعد
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            {!day.isReviewDay && (isLess || isMore) && (
              <div className={`text-center text-sm font-semibold rounded-xl py-3 px-4 mt-3 ${
                isLess
                  ? "bg-accent/10 text-accent border border-accent/20"
                  : "bg-primary/10 text-primary border border-primary/20"
              }`}>
                {isLess ? (
                  <><BookOpen className="w-4 h-4 inline me-1" /> تبقى {getDiffDisplay()}</>
                ) : (
                  <><Check className="w-4 h-4 inline me-1" /> أنجزت {getDiffDisplay()} زيادة</>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <DialogFooter className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)} className="flex-1 rounded-2xl font-semibold">
            إلغاء
          </Button>
          {!day.isOff && day.status !== "skipped" && onSkip && (
            <Button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSkip}
              className="flex-1 rounded-2xl bg-accent/15 text-accent hover:bg-accent/25 font-semibold"
            >
              <SkipForward className="w-4 h-4 me-1.5" /> تخطي اليوم
            </Button>
          )}
          {alreadyRecorded && (
            <Button type="button" onClick={handleUndo} className="flex-1 rounded-2xl bg-destructive/10 text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/20 font-semibold">
              <RotateCcw className="w-4 h-4 me-1.5" /> تراجع
            </Button>
          )}
          <Button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleSave}
            className="flex-1 rounded-2xl font-semibold bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary/90"
            disabled={calculatedVerses === 0}
          >
            <Check className="w-4 h-4 me-1.5" /> حفظ
          </Button>
        </DialogFooter>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}


--- FILE: /src/components/quran/ProgressRing.jsx ---

import React from "react";
import { toArabicNumbers } from "@/lib/quranPlanEngine";

export default function ProgressRing({ progress = 0, size = 160, strokeWidth = 10 }) {
  const radius = (size - strokeWidth) / 2;
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
  const offset = circumference - (progress / 100) * circumference;
  const center = size / 2;

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-flex items-center justify-center">
      <svg width={size} height={size} className="transform -rotate-90">
        <circle
          cx={center}
          cy={center}
          r={radius}
          stroke="hsl(var(--muted))"
          strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
          fill="none"
        />
        <circle
          cx={center}
          cy={center}
          r={radius}
          stroke="hsl(var(--primary))"
          strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
          fill="none"
          strokeLinecap="round"
          strokeDasharray={circumference}
          strokeDashoffset={offset}
          className="transition-all duration-1000 ease-out"
        />
      </svg>
      <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
        <span className="text-3xl font-bold font-display text-foreground">
          {toArabicNumbers(progress)}٪
        </span>
        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">مُنجَز</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


--- FILE: /src/components/quran/QuranDetailsPopover.jsx ---

// src/components/quran/QuranDetailsPopover.jsx
import React, { useMemo } from "react";
import { Info, BookOpen } from "lucide-react";
import { Popover, PopoverContent, PopoverTrigger } from "@/components/ui/popover";
import { getQuranData, getSurahList } from "@/lib/quranData";
import { versesToPages, toArabicNumbers } from "@/lib/quranPlanEngine";
import { getJuzBreakdown } from "@/lib/utils";

export const INFO_ICON_TRIGGER_CLASSES =
  "inline-flex items-center justify-center w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground " +
  "transition-colors duration-150 cursor-pointer shrink-0 select-none " +
  "[@media(hover:hover)]:hover:text-primary " +
  "data-[state=open]:text-primary " +
  "focus:outline-none focus-visible:outline-none [-webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent]";

export default function QuranDetailsPopover({ verses = 0, startPage = 1 }) {
  const details = useMemo(() => {
    const quranData = getQuranData();
    const surahList = quranData ? getSurahList() : [];
    const safeStart = Math.max(1, Math.min(604, startPage || 1));
    const totalPages = verses > 0 ? Math.max(1, versesToPages(verses, safeStart)) : 0;
    const safeEnd = totalPages > 0 ? Math.min(604, safeStart + totalPages - 1) : safeStart;

    const hizbCount = totalPages > 0 ? Math.max(1, Math.round((totalPages / 604) * 60)) : 0;
    const rubCount = totalPages > 0 ? Math.max(1, Math.round((totalPages / 604) * 240)) : 0;

    // ✅ حساب عدد الأجزاء بشكل رقمي متسق مع الأحزاب والأرباع (٣٠ للمصحف الكامل)
    let juzDisplay = "٠";
    if (totalPages > 0) {
      const { fullJuz, remainingPages } = getJuzBreakdown(totalPages, safeStart);
      if (remainingPages === 0 && fullJuz > 0) {
        juzDisplay = toArabicNumbers(fullJuz);
      } else {
        const approxJuz = (totalPages / 604) * 30;
        if (approxJuz < 0.15) {
          juzDisplay = "أقل من جزء";
        } else {
          const rounded = Math.round(approxJuz * 10) / 10;
          juzDisplay = Number.isInteger(rounded)
            ? toArabicNumbers(rounded)
            : toArabicNumbers(rounded.toFixed(1)).replace(".", "٫");
        }
      }
    }

    const surahsInRange = totalPages > 0
      ? surahList.filter((s) => s.endPage >= safeStart && s.startPage <= safeEnd)
      : [];

    const startJuz = quranData?.pageMap?.[safeStart]?.juzu || 1;
    const endJuz = quranData?.pageMap?.[safeEnd]?.juzu || 1;

    return {
      verses,
      totalPages,
      juzDisplay,
      hizbCount,
      rubCount,
      surahsInRange,
      startJuz,
      endJuz,
      safeStart,
      safeEnd,
    };
  }, [verses, startPage]);

  if (!verses || verses <= 0) return null;

  const remainingSurahs = Math.max(0, details.surahsInRange.length - 8);

  return (
    <Popover>
      <PopoverTrigger asChild>
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label="تفاصيل النطاق"
          className={INFO_ICON_TRIGGER_CLASSES}
        >
          <Info className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[1.75]" />
        </button>
      </PopoverTrigger>
      <PopoverContent side="bottom" align="center" className="w-72 p-3.5 rounded-2xl text-xs" dir="rtl">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pb-2.5 mb-2.5 border-b border-border/50">
          <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4 text-primary shrink-0" />
          <span className="font-bold text-sm text-foreground">تفاصيل النطاق</span>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-muted-foreground">الآيات</span>
            <span className="font-bold text-foreground">{toArabicNumbers(details.verses)}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-muted-foreground">الصفحات</span>
            <span className="font-bold text-foreground">{toArabicNumbers(details.totalPages)}</span>
          </div>

          <div className="border-t border-border/40 my-1.5" />

          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-muted-foreground">الأجزاء</span>
            <span className="font-bold text-foreground">{details.juzDisplay}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-muted-foreground">الأحزاب</span>
            <span className="font-bold text-foreground">{toArabicNumbers(details.hizbCount)}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-muted-foreground">الأرباع</span>
            <span className="font-bold text-foreground">{toArabicNumbers(details.rubCount)}</span>
          </div>

          <div className="border-t border-border/40 my-1.5" />

          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5">
              <span className="text-muted-foreground">عدد السور</span>
              <span className="font-bold text-foreground">{toArabicNumbers(details.surahsInRange.length)}</span>
            </div>
            {details.surahsInRange.length > 0 && (
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                {details.surahsInRange.slice(0, 8).map((s) => (
                  <span
                    key={s.id}
                    className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-primary/10 text-primary font-medium text-[11px]"
                  >
                    {s.name}
                  </span>
                ))}
                {/* ✅ عرض عدد السور المتبقية بدقة بدلاً من عبارة ...وغيرها */}
                {remainingSurahs > 0 && (
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-muted text-muted-foreground font-medium text-[11px]">
                    <span dir="ltr">+{toArabicNumbers(remainingSurahs)}</span>
                    <span>أخرى</span>
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="border-t border-border/40 my-1.5" />

          {/* ✅ مسافات واضحة ومتوازنة بين الحرف والرقم في سطري البداية والنهاية */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-muted-foreground">من (جزء/صفحة)</span>
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 font-semibold text-foreground">
              <span>ج{"\u00A0"}{toArabicNumbers(details.startJuz)}</span>
              <span className="text-muted-foreground">·</span>
              <span>ص{"\u00A0"}{toArabicNumbers(details.safeStart)}</span>
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-muted-foreground">إلى (جزء/صفحة)</span>
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 font-semibold text-foreground">
              <span>ج{"\u00A0"}{toArabicNumbers(details.endJuz)}</span>
              <span className="text-muted-foreground">·</span>
              <span>ص{"\u00A0"}{toArabicNumbers(details.safeEnd)}</span>
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </PopoverContent>
    </Popover>
  );
}


--- FILE: /src/components/quran/ScheduleView.jsx ---

// src/components/quran/ScheduleView.jsx
import React, { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import DayCard from "./DayCard";
import { ChevronDown, ChevronUp, CalendarDays } from "lucide-react";
import { getLocalToday, toArabicNumbers } from "@/lib/quranPlanEngine";

function formatArabicDaysCount(count) {
  if (count === 1) return "يوم واحد";
  if (count === 2) return "يومان";
  const ar = toArabicNumbers(count);
  const mod100 = count % 100;
  if (mod100 >= 3 && mod100 <= 10) return `${ar} أيام`;
  return `${ar} يوماً`;
}

export default function ScheduleView({ schedule, onRecordDay, onQuickComplete, planType = "review" }) {
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState("default");
  const today = getLocalToday();

  const futureDays = useMemo(() => {
    return schedule.filter((day) => day.date >= today);
  }, [schedule, today]);

  // ✅ عرض ٤ أيام افتراضياً و٧ أيام عند الضغط على "عرض المزيد"
  const defaultLimit = 4;
  const moreLimit = 7;

  const displayDays = useMemo(() => {
    const limit = viewMode === "default" ? defaultLimit : moreLimit;
    return futureDays.slice(0, limit);
  }, [futureDays, viewMode]);

  const displayedCount = displayDays.length;
  const remainingFutureDays = Math.max(0, futureDays.length - displayedCount);
  const canToggleMore = futureDays.length > defaultLimit;

  const toggleView = () => {
    setViewMode((prev) => (prev === "default" ? "more" : "default"));
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <CalendarDays className="w-5 h-5 text-primary" />
          <h3 className="text-lg font-bold font-heading text-foreground">الجدول اليومي</h3>
        </div>

        {canToggleMore && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={toggleView}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-primary/15 bg-primary/5 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-primary transition-colors hover:bg-primary/10 hover:text-primary"
          >
            {viewMode === "default" ? "عرض المزيد" : "عرض أقل"}
            {viewMode === "default" ? (
              <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[2.2]" />
            ) : (
              <ChevronUp className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[2.2]" />
            )}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {displayDays.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-8 text-muted-foreground">
          لا توجد أيام متبقية في الخطة
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
          {displayDays.map((day) => (
            <DayCard
              key={day.date}
              day={day}
              isToday={day.date === today}
              onRecord={onRecordDay}
              onQuickComplete={onQuickComplete}
              planType={planType}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {displayedCount > 0 && (
        <div className="text-center mt-4">
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            {remainingFutureDays === 0
              ? `يُعرَض ${formatArabicDaysCount(displayedCount)} (كامل المتبقي في الخطة)`
              : `يُعرَض ${formatArabicDaysCount(displayedCount)} · باقي ${formatArabicDaysCount(remainingFutureDays)} في الخطة`}
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import React from "react";

export default function StatCard({ icon: Icon, label, value, subValue, className = "" }) {
  return (
    <div className={`bg-card rounded-2xl border border-border/60 p-5 flex items-start gap-4 transition-all duration-300 hover:shadow-md hover:border-primary/20 max-md:items-start ${className}`}>
      <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-primary/10 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 max-md:mt-0.5">
        <Icon className="w-5 h-5 text-primary" />
      </div>
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground leading-relaxed">{label}</p>
        <p className="text-xl font-bold text-foreground mt-0.5">{value}</p>
        {subValue && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">{subValue}</p>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

--- FILE: /src/components/ui/arabic-date-picker.tsx ---

// src/components/ui/arabic-date-picker.tsx
import * as React from "react";
import { format, parse } from "date-fns";
import { arSA } from "date-fns/locale/ar-SA";
import { DayPicker } from "react-day-picker";
import "react-day-picker/dist/style.css";

import {
  Popover,
  PopoverContent,
  PopoverTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/popover";
import { CalendarIcon, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const arabicDigits = ['٠', '١', '٢', '٣', '٤', '٥', '٦', '٧', '٨', '٩'];
const toArabicManual = (num: number): string => {
  return num.toString().split('').map(d => arabicDigits[parseInt(d)]).join('');
};

interface ArabicDatePickerProps {
  value: string;
  onChange: (date: string) => void;
  placeholder?: string;
  className?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export function ArabicDatePicker({
  value,
  onChange,
  placeholder = "اختر تاريخاً",
  className,
  disabled = false,
}: ArabicDatePickerProps) {
  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);

  const selectedDate = value ? parse(value, "yyyy-MM-dd", new Date()) : undefined;

  const handleSelect = (date: Date | undefined) => {
    if (date) {
      const localDate = new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), date.getDate());
      onChange(format(localDate, "yyyy-MM-dd"));
    } else {
      onChange("");
    }
    setOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <Popover open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
      <PopoverTrigger asChild>
        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={disabled}
          className={cn(
            // ✅ كلاسات ثابتة، بدون isActive — يعتمد على open فقط
            "group w-full min-h-[40px] px-3 rounded-xl text-base leading-none",
            "flex items-center gap-2 text-right",
            "border border-input bg-transparent",
            "transition-colors duration-150",
            "hover:border-primary",
            "focus-visible:border-primary",
            "data-[state=open]:border-primary",
            !value && "text-muted-foreground group-hover:text-foreground",
            disabled && "opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed",
            className
          )}
          style={{
            minHeight: '40px',
            height: '40px',
            paddingTop: '0',
            paddingBottom: '0',
          }}
        >
          <CalendarIcon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
          <span className="flex-1 truncate" style={{ lineHeight: '24px', fontSize: '15px' }}>
            {value && selectedDate ? (
              <span
                dir="rtl"
                style={{
                  display: 'inline-flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  gap: '0.35rem',
                }}
              >
                <span>{toArabicManual(selectedDate.getDate())}</span>
                <span>{format(selectedDate, "MMMM", { locale: arSA })}</span>
                <span>{toArabicManual(selectedDate.getFullYear())}</span>
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span>{placeholder}</span>
            )}
          </span>
        </button>
      </PopoverTrigger>
      {/* @ts-expect-error - Radix UI PopoverContent types issue with children */}
      <PopoverContent className="w-auto p-0" align="start" sideOffset={4}>
        <div dir="rtl">
          <DayPicker
            mode="single"
            selected={selectedDate}
            onSelect={handleSelect}
            locale={arSA}
            formatters={{
              formatDay: (date) => toArabicManual(date.getDate()),
              formatWeekdayName: (date) => format(date, "EEEEEE", { locale: arSA }),
            }}
            components={{
              CaptionLabel: ({ displayMonth }: { displayMonth: Date; id?: string }) => (
                <span
                  dir="rtl"
                  style={{
                    display: 'inline-flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    gap: '0.5rem',
                    fontWeight: 'bold',
                    fontSize: '1rem',
                  }}
                >
                  <span>{format(displayMonth, "MMMM", { locale: arSA })}</span>
                  <span>{toArabicManual(displayMonth.getFullYear())}</span>
                </span>
              ),
              IconLeft: () => <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" />,
              IconRight: () => <ChevronLeft className="h-4 w-4" />,
            }}
            styles={{
              caption: {
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                paddingBottom: '0.5rem',
              },
              head_cell: { fontWeight: "bold" },
            }}
            modifiersClassNames={{
              selected: "bg-primary text-primary-foreground rounded-md",
              today: "border border-primary",
            }}
          />
        </div>
      </PopoverContent>
    </Popover>
  );
}

  {
    variants: {
      variant: {

          "bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow-md hover:bg-primary/90",
        destructive:
          "bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground shadow-sm hover:bg-destructive/90",
        outline:
          "border border-input bg-transparent shadow-sm hover:bg-primary/10 hover:text-primary transition-colors",
        secondary:
          "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground shadow-sm hover:bg-secondary/80",
        ghost: "hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground",
        link: "text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
